## Bespoke restyle, chat 3: the two way steps (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Built from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 3 on build-your-programme.html, steps 01 (Live-in or daytime), 03 (Day or night) and 04 (Meals). The engine, the rules, build-your-programme.js, the copy, header and footer are unchanged; every name, value and data-* hook in plan section 5 is kept.
- **Each step is What We Do's two stop line (Device A, the .dnl recipe).** The option cards (.choices, .choice) are gone from these three steps; in their place a .dkl block: the two option titles as the end labels (Montserrat 500, .8125rem, .16em uppercase, ink; phones .14em), the hairline track (--tline-track), the ember fill (--tline-ink) and the --ttip circle sliding to the chosen end over 650ms on --tease, a --tdot stop at each end (the far stop .45 until reached). The chosen end's label at opacity 1, the other at .5. The chosen option's second line fades in under the line in Karla, var(--ink-soft), over 300ms (the left option's under the left end, the right option's right aligned under the right end); the other fades out and leaves the accessibility tree (visibility).
- **The radios stay the source of truth.** Both radios stay in the DOM inside their end label, visually hidden with .sr-only (never display:none), each with an id and aria-describedby on its second line. The line is pure CSS reading the checked radio through :has(), so changes the engine makes itself (night forced back to day, Restore, the budget's Apply) move the circle too. Tapping an end label, or either half of the line (two 44px tall <label for> targets), is a native label click on the radio, so the engine gets its usual change event. Arrow keys on the focused radio move the choice and the circle follows; the focus ring is drawn on the end label. No script change was needed.
- **Night disabled with daytime in-home care:** the Day and night end at opacity .3 with pointer-events none, its half of the line untappable, its stop at .3; the data-night-band note still shown. data-night-choice moved onto the night end label; data-meals-sub is on the Yes line's text, which the script still rewrites. The night note itself lost its tinted haze box and is plain Karla on the ground (Claude, following chat 2's treatment of the start page note).
- **Measured** (lab/bespoke-twoway-check.mjs, 1440x900 and 390x844): for all three steps, tapping each end label and each half of the line checks the right radio (format in/out, shift day/night, meals yes/no) and the circle's centre lands on that end's stop (0.00px off); fees as before: format out £1,995 to £2,145, shift night to £2,016, meals no to £1,811, and back to £1,995 each time. ArrowLeft and ArrowRight on the focused radio change the value and the fee and the circle follows (0.00px). Night case: night chosen, then daytime in-home on 01, step 03 is forced back to day, night disabled, end opacity .3, untappable (fee stays £2,145), note shown; back on live-in, night opens again. End labels 44px tall and one line at both widths (at 390: Live-in care 111px, Daytime in-home care 203px); line halves 44 by 313 at 1440 (640px line) and 44 by 164 at 390. No sideways overflow.
- **Checks.** programme-check 49 of 49; deck-check ok at both widths; bespoke-check all UI cases match (it drives the radios directly, so no change was needed); bespoke-phone clean; sync-header and sync-footer --check ok on every page. None of the four checks needed updating.
- **Meals (Claude's judgement, for TJ).** It looks clean and matches 01 and 03, but the metaphor is wrong for a yes or no: the line reads as a progression, and choosing "No meals" fills it in ember and reaches the far stop, so taking something away looks like going further. On 01 and 03 the two ends really are two kinds of care, so the line fits. Recommended fallback, not built: two Device D rows with the filling dot (chat 4's .dk-dot), Yes, cook for us and No meals, each with its second line in Karla. Awaiting TJ.
- **Tools and backups.** lab/bespoke-twoway-check.mjs <outdir> (taps, keys, night case, measurements, screenshots); lab/bespoke-twoway-shots.mjs <outdir>. Screenshots: lab/bespoke-restyle-3/ (step01, step03, step04 at 1440 and 390, plus -b chosen states, -focus and step03 -disabled); before: lab/bespoke-restyle-3/before/. Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-3.html.bak and .css.bak (no js change, so no js backup).

## Bespoke restyle, chat 4: How long and the dot control (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Built from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 4 on build-your-programme.html, step 02. The engine, the rules, build-your-programme.js, the copy, header and footer are unchanged; step 04 (Meals) untouched. Every name, value and hook in plan section 5 is kept.
- **How long is Tailored's day line (Device A, four stops).** The four cards (.lengths, .length) are gone. In their place a .dkd block: 5, 7, 14, 30 as Instrument Serif numerals above the stops (clamp(1.8rem, min(3.4vw, 5vh), 3.1rem), opacity .38 at rest, 1 when chosen; phones clamp(1.9rem, 8.2vw, 2.6rem)), DAYS at the left in the unit style (Montserrat 500, .66rem, .18em, uppercase, .7; phones .8125rem), the hairline track, the ember fill and the --ttip circle sliding on --tease over 650ms, a --tdot stop per stay, solid once reached and .45 before. Under each numeral nothing.
- **The radios stay the source of truth.** The four radios (name days, values 5, 7, 14, 30) stay in the DOM inside their numeral label, visually hidden with .sr-only. Pure CSS reads the checked radio through :has(), as chat 3, so the engine's own changes (Restore, the budget's Apply) move the circle too. Tapping a numeral, or a quarter of the line (four 44px tall label targets), is a native label click, so the engine gets its usual change event. Arrow keys on the focused radio move the stay and the circle follows; the focus ring is drawn on the numeral.

- **The row under the line.** One row in Karla, var(--ink-soft), for the chosen stay: "The first week home · Complete programme £1,995" (the existing .length__sub and .length__price spans, now plain Karla; the middle dot is drawn in CSS, as the plan's example shows). Four rows stacked in one grid cell cross fade over 300ms. The pound figure fades rather than rolls (Claude: rolling it would need script and the ticker already is the fee).
- **The dot control, .dk-dot (defined, not used on the page yet).** 11px circle (--tdot), 1px ring in --tline-track at rest; selected (a checked input inside it, or a checked input just before it, or aria-checked true): ember fill, ring gone, 200ms --ease-out; 44px tap target; focus ring 2px ember on :focus-visible, disabled at .3. For chats 5 and 6 (and Meals if it becomes rows). The row's hairline goes where the dot is.
- **Measured** (lab/bespoke-howlong-check.mjs, 1440x900 and 390x844): for each stay, tapping its numeral and tapping its part of the line checks the right radio (5, 7, 14, 30) and the circle's centre and the fill's end land on that stay's stop (0.00 to 0.01px off, x and y); the row under the line reads that stay's sub and price text, one row shown; the ticker fee is £1,995, £2,495, £4,495, £6,399. Stops 171.92px apart at 1440 (89.6px at 390), numerals centred on stops, line quarters 44px tall, no sideways overflow. ArrowRight and ArrowLeft on the focused radio walk 5, 7, 14, 30, 14 with the circle on the stop and the fee following. Screenshots: lab/bespoke-restyle-4/step02-1440.png and step02-390.png (and per stay, step02-<w>-<stay>.png).

- **Tools and backups.** lab/bespoke-howlong-check.mjs <outdir>. Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-4.html.bak and .css.bak (no js change), lab/deck-check.before-restyle-4.mjs.bak. The old .length card rules were removed from the stylesheet; the shared control lists at the top (.choice, .length, ...) still name .length harmlessly.

## Bespoke restyle, chat 5: treatments and massage rhythm (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Built from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 5 on build-your-programme.html, steps 05 and 06. The engine, the rules, the copy, header and footer are unchanged; step 04 (Meals) untouched. Every name, value and hook in plan section 5 is kept.
- **Device D rows with the dot.** The six .treat labels and the rhythm rows drawn into [data-rhythms] are now hairline rows: no thumbnail (the six img elements are removed from the markup), no background, no border except a 1px var(--hairline) above every row but the first. Each row is a 44px column holding the .dk-dot (defined in chat 4) and a body column. The dot is the row's mark and the whole row is the tap target (a label). The checkboxes and radios stay in the DOM with .sr-only, straight before their .dk-dot, so the dot fills ember from input:checked + .dk-dot and the keyboard, the engine's change events and applyRules are untouched.

Herbal bath and Belly binding show INCLUDED in mono uppercase on the right and have no dot (their text lines up with the others). The old haze badge on INCLUDED is gone.
- **Massage rhythm.** drawRhythms now emits the same recipe: radio (.sr-only), .dk-dot, then a head with the option title and, on the standard option only, STANDARD in mono, and the existing sub line in Karla. This is the only script change (the markup string); nothing else in build-your-programme.js moved. The word STANDARD is the plan's own label (chat 5 and section 4); there was no such word on the page before, so it is the one new visible word (Claude, from the plan).
- **Disabled.** With massage unticked, applyRules still disables every rhythm radio and .step.is-off dims the step to .4. The rows no longer dim a second time (they did before, giving .16); the dots follow the step's .4. The rhythm note still reads "Postpartum massage isn't selected, so there's no rhythm to set..."
- **Measured** (lab/bespoke-treat-check.mjs, 1440x900 and 390x844). Base fee 5 days £1,995. Unticking each treatment then ticking it back: massage £1,802 (count Not selected, back to 3 sessions), scrub £1,963, reflex £1,969, facial £1,969, herbal foot £1,975, thermal £1,972, each returning to £1,995 with the count back (3 sessions, then 1 time each). Rhythm options on 5 days: 3 massages (STANDARD) £1,995, 2 massages £1,931. Rows have a transparent background and no side or bottom border (only the top hairline, none on the first row), 44px dot target, row height 64 to 65px at 1440 (up to 104px at 390 where the massage name wraps), no img in any row. Unticked massage disables both rhythm radios; re-ticking enables them. No sideways overflow. The last treatment row clears the bottom bar when the page is scrolled to its end (1440: row bottom 756 against bar top 810; 390: 648 against 699).

## Bespoke restyle, chat 6: What matters most (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Built from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 6 on build-your-programme.html, step 07. The engine, the rules, build-your-programme.js, the copy, header and footer are unchanged; every name, value and data-* hook in plan section 5 is kept (the six p-* checkboxes, their values, .prio input and [data-prio-count]).
- **Six rows, Device D with the dot.** The six .prio cards are now the step 05 and 06 row recipe: no background, no border, a 1px var(--hairline) above every row except the first in each column, a 44px column holding the .dk-dot, and the body beside it. The checkbox is class sr-only, placed straight before its .dk-dot, so the dot fills ember from input:checked + .dk-dot and the keyboard focus ring is drawn on the dot.
- **Type.** Title in Montserrat 500 .8125rem (.875rem at 620px and under). The line under it in Karla .8125rem, var(--ink-soft), 2px under the title. Copy unchanged.
- **Layout.** Desktop: grid-template-columns repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)), column gap 48px, row gap 0 (measured at 308px per column at 1440). At max-width 860px: one column, and the hairline sits above the second row only, so the first row has none.
- **Removed.** The old card rules for .prio (border, haze, blush when checked, hover border), the flex .priorities, the .matters .prio paper background, and the phone one column rules that the card version needed. .prio__count (the "Three things noted" line) is kept as it was.
- **Measured** (lab/bespoke-restyle-6-check.mjs, 1440x900 and 390x844): ticking p-sleep, p-baby and p-house checks those three and only those; their dots fill ember (rgb(166, 76, 69)) and the other three stay empty; every .prio has a transparent background and zero left, right and bottom border; the top hairlines are 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1 px at 1440 and 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1 px at 390; no sideways overflow on the document or the step; the count line shows "Three things noted for your call."
- **Checks.** programme-check all passed; deck-check passed (it has no .prio selector, so nothing needed updating); bespoke-check all UI cases match; bespoke-phone clean; bespoke-treat-check all passed; sync-header and sync-footer --check ok on every page.
- **Screenshots.** lab/bespoke-restyle-6/step07-1440.png and step07-390.png. At 390 the fixed bottom bar sits over the element capture's lower edge, which is the bar itself, not the rows.
- **Backups.** lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-6.html.bak and lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-6.css.bak. No JS change, so no js backup. Not committed or pushed.

## Bespoke restyle, chat 7: the summary (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Built from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 7 on build-your-programme.html (data-section builder-summary). The engine, the rules, build-your-programme.js, the copy (beyond the two removals below), header and footer are unchanged; step 04 (Meals) untouched; every name, value, data-* hook and id in plan section 5 is kept.
- **The fee is glass merged into the start page's photograph (Device C).** The summary opens with the same photograph (s3-rest.webp) and the fee block reuses .dk-start__copy and .dk-start__frame, so it carries the very same ::before frost and nine stop mask (sideways on desktop, upward on phones). It holds "Indicative fee", the figure (.dk-sumfee, Instrument Serif, ember, data-count-fee), the introductory pricing line and the two notes (data-complete-notice, data-floor-notice, data-reserve-note), now plain Karla (.dk-note), no tinted boxes. Claude's calls: a wider 5 to 4 crop of the photograph on desktop, and the copy's left padding is 0 so its text lines up with the headings below.
- **Every other .dk-card is text on the ground.** No background, no border, no radius, no padding sides; a 1px hairline drawn by a ::before (so the computed border is 0) above each, and the existing .dk-h2 capitals under it. What you have built: Device D rows (1px left hairline, 20px, label in Montserrat 500 .7rem .16em uppercase, body in Karla); the Change links are Device E (Montserrat 500 .72rem .16em, 1px underline at 5px, 44px tall). The ember changed flag now draws on the row's hairline. Day strip: unchanged marks, on the ground. Always included: Device D rows. Budget: the field is an underline (no box, no paper), the recommendation (.fit) a 1px ember left rule with Karla text, and its Apply buttons are Device E links (they were btn btn--outline btn--sm). Ready to talk: both actions are Device E links; the enquiry brief text (data-brief-text) lost its canvas panel and sits behind a left hairline in mono.
- **Request my consultation** is the same action as the bar's Book a call (both go to the Calendly consultation), so by the plan it is now an underlined link (a.dk-link), href kept. Copy my programme summary is a Device E link; data-brief-copy and its Copied label are untouched.
- **The small spaced capitals YOUR PROGRAMME label above the summary is removed** (orchestrator, plan section 4; the bar's step name and number already say it). Claude's call.
- **The dead phone .feebar is removed, markup and CSS (orchestrator; Claude confirmed it was dead).** The script never reads feebar; the only class that showed it, is-fee-adrift, is added by nothing in js/ or the HTML; the script only collects [data-fee], and the screen reader span with data-fee in the ticker remains, so the fee text still updates. The .feebar rules, its barin keyframes and the feebar half of the settle selectors are gone. Old tools lab/programme-panel.mjs and lab/programme-contrast.mjs still look for .feebar and were already stale (they predate the deck).
- **Button count.** The page now has one .btn--primary element in the whole document, the bar's Next, which the script toggles to ember on page 0 (Begin) and page 8 (Book a call) and to the underlined link on pages 1 to 7. So "two" means those two states of that one button; no other element carries btn--primary or btn--outline anywhere.
- **Measured** (lab/bespoke-summary-check.mjs, 1440x900 and 390x844): every former .dk-card computes a transparent background and 0 border on all four sides (the fee card's frost is its ::before); no .eyebrow; no .btn--outline; no .feebar; sumfee equals the ticker (£4,495 for 14 days); no sideways overflow; the bar says Book a call. Change links 1, 3, 4, 5 and 7 each land on their step. Budget 2500 shows the fit "Our complete seven day programme fits that" and one Apply (44px tall, no background or border, underlined); applying it sets 7 days, goes to step 02, and the fee reads £2,495 in both the summary and the ticker. Copy my programme summary reads Copied, the clipboard text equals data-brief-text (819 characters; the browser clipboard adds CRs, which the check strips), and the label returns after 1.8s.
- **Checks.** programme-check all passed; deck-check ok; bespoke-check all UI cases match; bespoke-phone clean; bespoke-twoway-check passed (one timing failure on one run, clean on rerun); bespoke-howlong-check all ok; bespoke-treat-check all passed; sync-header and sync-footer --check ok on every page. No existing check read the old summary selectors except the .dk-sumfee text read, which still exists, so none needed updating.
- **Tools, screenshots and backups.** lab/bespoke-summary-check.mjs <outdir>. Screenshots: lab/bespoke-restyle-7/ (summary-1440-top, -s1 to -s4, -hero, -budget; summary-390-top, -s1 to -s6, -hero, -budget). Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-7.html.bak and .css.bak (no js change). Not committed or pushed.

## Bespoke restyle, chat 8: phones and short windows (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
Phones pass and short window pass on build-your-programme.html, from BESPOKE-RESTYLE-PLAN.md chat 8. Measured with lab/bespoke-walk8.mjs (all nine pages at five sizes; sideways overflow of the document and of each page's own content, every visible link, button, label and radio under 44px, the deck's height). Backup: lab/build-your-programme.before-restyle-8.css.bak. Engine, rules, copy, header, footer, step 04 (Meals) and every name, value, data hook and id are untouched. Only css/build-your-programme.css changed.

Measurements before the fixes (the same after, except where a fix is listed):

| Size | Document overflow | Page content overflow | Deck height (all nine pages) | Ticker band | Bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 390x844 | 0 | 0 | 541 | 57 | 145 |
| 360x640 | 0 | 0 | 337 | 57 | 145 |
| 430x932 | 0 | 0 | 629 | 57 | 145 |
| 1835x605 | 0 | 0 | 344 | 69 | 91 |
| 1280x640 | 0 | 0 | 383 | 69 | 87 |

The deck stays at least 320px tall at both short desktop sizes (344 and 383), so the ticker band was not reduced. The deck is 337 at 360x640, also above 320. (The only element wider than its page was the aurora field, which is meant to bleed and is clipped.)

Tap targets under 44px, and what was done:
- Back and Next links: 41x44 and 39x44 (their words are narrower than 44). Fixed: .dk-link min-width 44px, Next aligned to the right edge so the underline stays under the word. Now 44 or more everywhere.
- How long numerals (.dkd__day) on the short desktop windows: 44x34 at 1835x605 and 44x36 at 1280x640. Fixed: min-height 44px. (Phones were already 44 or more.)
- Left as is: the label "Have a care budget in mind?" on the summary (23px tall). It is a heading whose field (44px) is beside it, not a control to aim at.
- Stops on the bar are 44px wide each; on a 360 screen the stops sit 39px apart, so neighbouring targets overlap by about 5px (the later stop wins the overlap). Reached stops and the next one are the only live ones. Not changed.

Other fixes:
- At 360 wide the two way labels wrapped to two lines (Live-in care, Daytime in-home care; Daytime care, Day and night care). Fixed by tightening tracking, not type: at 380px and under .dkl__end letter spacing .09em (was .14em) and the gap 10px. Both labels now sit on one line at 360, 390 and 430. The How long numerals and the DAYS line fit at every phone size.
- Budget field (Claude's call, via the orchestrator): the native number spinner is hidden with ::-webkit-inner-spin-button and ::-webkit-outer-spin-button (appearance none, margin 0) and -moz-appearance: textfield. The input is still type number, with the same min, step and behaviour.
- Merged glass: looked at the start page and the summary photograph at 360, 390 and 430 wide; the frost fades into the picture with no edge, text clear. No change needed.

Tests: programme-check (all passed), deck-check, bespoke-check (all UI cases match), bespoke-phone, bespoke-twoway-check, bespoke-howlong-check, bespoke-treat-check, bespoke-summary-check all pass; sync-header and sync-footer --check ok on every page. Final screenshots of all nine pages at 1440x900 and 390x844 are in lab/bespoke-restyle-final/; the walk's screenshots at the five sizes are in lab/bespoke-restyle-8/.

## Bespoke Programmes: one flowing page, no scroll inside a scroll (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
TJ: he does not like the scroll inside the scroll at all. Cause: the spec's carousel was a fixed frame (ticker, deck, bar) whose pages each scrolled on their own; the start page and the summary never fit, so they always scrolled inside the frame. Built (one thing at a time, TJ's instruction; the bar's look, the Begin button and page one's content are still to be discussed):
- css/build-your-programme.css: the frame is a normal page; the steps are sections down it (.dk-deck and .dk-page no longer scroll or snap); the fee band is sticky under the header; the bar is fixed at the foot, and the page keeps the bar's height free below the footer (.page--deck padding, --dk-bar-h set on the root); sections land under the header and fee band (scroll-margin and the script's landY).
- js/build-your-programme.js deckController: the section being read is the last whose top has passed 45 percent of the window (the last once the page reaches its end), and the line, the step name and Next follow it as the window scrolls; Next, Back, the stops, the arrow keys and the summary's Change links scroll the page to a section with the page's own eased glide (instant steps toward a target read each frame, 500 to 1100ms; a touch, wheel or key stops it), not the browser's smooth scroll; every stop is tappable, since every section is on the page; the sideways deck code (swipe settle, resize re snap, inert pages) is gone.
- Found on the way: the start page's blurred aurora, now as tall as a wide section, took 2.2s to paint at 1835x605 and froze the scroll; it is now fixed to the window (one window sized aurora behind every section).
Checked (lab/bespoke-flow-check.mjs, lab/bespoke-foot-check.mjs, lab/bespoke-frame-probe.mjs, lab/bespoke-land-probe.mjs) at 1440x900, 390x844 and 1835x605: no element scrolls on its own; no sideways overflow; scrolling down, the bar reads 0 to 8 in order; Next lands every section exactly under the fee band (0px); a stop tap goes back to 02; at the end the bar reads Book a call and the footer's foot meets the bar's top exactly; no frame over 50ms during a glide. All lab checks pass (programme, deck, bespoke, phone, two way, how long, treatments, summary) and both sync checks. lab/bespoke-twoway-check.mjs now reaches a step by scrolling the window; deck-check, frame-shots, howlong, treat, summary and restyle-6 checks still contain the old sideways move but pass (they also tap), to tidy later. Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-flow.{html,css,js}.bak, lab/deck-check.before-flow.mjs.bak, lab/bespoke-twoway-check.before-flow.mjs.bak.

## Bespoke Programmes: the flowing page reverted (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
TJ: one continuous scroll is exactly what he is trying to avoid. The held frame is back (css and js restored from lab/build-your-programme.before-flow.*.bak; the flowing versions kept in lab/flow-attempt/). TJ's direction instead: everything fits one screen per step, with economic spacing; no step numbers; the fee, Back and Next and the progress line in one layer at the foot (the top fee band goes); the title sized for the space; more screens where a step will not fit. And a new bar (TJ): only the previous, current and next stops visible, the line sliding past a fixed current point as in A Day With Us, with a definite beginning and a definite end.

## Bespoke Programmes: the bar, one layer with the sliding line (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
TJ's decisions (phase 1 of the redesign): "One layer at the foot": the top fee band goes; the fee, Back and Next and the progress line all live in the bar, with no step numbers anywhere in the bar (the 00 to 08 row goes). The line shows only three stops, "the previous step, the current step, and the next step greyed out"; the rest of the line runs off out of view; moving from step to step the line slides as in A Day With Us; a definite beginning at the start (nothing left of the first stop) and a definite end at the last step (nothing right of the last stop). TJ found the row of nine dots jam packed. The deck, its pages, steps and summary are unchanged (phase 2); Begin stays the ember button for now (TJ wants it changed later).

Built (the details below are Claude's calls):
- **The frame** is now the deck (1fr) and the bar (auto). The band's markup moved into the bar unchanged in its hooks: .dk-fee (now the bar's fee group, data-section builder-fee kept), .dk-fee__label, .dk-fee__amount with [data-count-fee], the sr-only [data-fee] live region and [data-fee-chip]. counter(), chip() and the engine are untouched; the roll and the chip work as before. The note "Introductory pricing, held for 2026" is dropped from the bar (it did not fit; it is on the summary and in the legal line).
- **Desktop, one row:** the fee (label over the figure, the chip beside the figure) in a fixed 13rem column, Back (4rem), the line (1fr), Next (11.5rem). Fixed columns, so the line never changes length between pages (432px at 1440 and 1835). Bar 77px tall at both (was 90 plus the 69px band).
- **Phones, two rows:** the fee on the left with Back and Next on the right, then the line full width (342px at 390). Bar 128px (was 145 plus the 57px band). The change chip sits under the figure on phones, level with the step name, because beside the figure it ran into Back on the See my programme page. At 380px and under the bar's gaps are 10px and the links' tracking .12em, because at 360 the figure touched Back on that page.
- **The sliding line:** a clipped window (.dk-line__win, overflow hidden, 44px tall, a container so the pitch is 34cqw) onto a track that starts at the first stop and ends at the last. Stops sit at an even pitch of 34 percent of the window, so the previous and next sit 16 percent in from the edges and every other stop falls outside. The window's edges fade over 12 percent each side (mask gradient). The current stop is held at the centre under the --ttip circle, the step name (data-title) above it in Montserrat 500 .66rem .18em uppercase, .7 (phones .75rem). The previous is a --tdot solid on the ember fill; the next a 7px faint dot (scaled --tdot, .45) on the grey track. On Back and Next the track translates by one pitch over 650ms on --tease and the fill scales with it, so the fill's end never leaves the centre; the arriving stop grows and fills as it lands, the new next fades in, the old previous fades out. A Change link or any jump slides the whole distance in one movement. Stops beyond previous and next fade and go visibility hidden. Only the previous and next dots are live buttons (44px, aria-label "Go to <title>", tabbable; the current has aria-current step); after a keyboard press on one, focus moves on to the stop now in the same place. Arrow keys, swipe, snap, inert pages unchanged. Reduced motion: no transitions. data-back and data-next and their labels unchanged; --pos is still set on [data-line] (now with --i and --last).

Measured (lab/bespoke-bar2-check.mjs, 1440x900, 390x844, 1835x605 and 360x640; 204 checks, all passed):
- On every page exactly the previous, current and next stops are visible inside the window and unmasked (page 0: 0 and 1; page 8: 7 and 8). The current stop's dot, the circle and the step name sit on the window's centre (0.00 to 0.02px off) and the fill ends there; prev and next are 146.9px either side at 1440 and 1835 and 116.3px at 390. Page 0: the rail starts at the centre and the fill is empty; page 8: the rail ends at the centre. Tabbable stops are exactly prev and next, 44x44.
- One Next slide (1 to 2) stepped through with the transitions paused at 0 to 650ms: the track goes from -146.9 to -293.8px on the ease, the fill's end stays at 0.0px at every frame, three stops in view at rest, the next fading in while the previous slides off at the left edge.
- Change link to 01 from the summary lands on 01 with 0, 1, 2 visible; tapping the next and previous dots and Enter on the focused next dot all go one step. The fee rolls and the chip shows (+ £2,500) on How long 5 to 14 (£1,995 to £4,495). The widest bar (See my programme, £6,399, chip showing) has no overlaps at all four sizes. No sideways overflow.
- Deck height, before and after: 1440x900 640 to 722; 390x844 541 to 615; 1835x605 344 to 427; 360x640 337 to 411.

Found on the way (Claude): in headless Chrome (software raster) the taller deck can stall a frame by 1 to 2.5s on desktop the first time the summary comes into view; it goes away when the frame is cut back to the old height and is not caused by the bar's mask, glass or transitions. In a real Chrome window with the GPU (lab/bespoke-bar2-stall-probe.mjs with GPU=1 HEADED=1) there is no stall at either desktop size (every jump lands in 100 to 160ms). Two checks timed out on that stall with fixed waits: lab/bespoke-twoway-check.mjs (its go now waits until the deck lands on the page) and lab/bespoke-summary-check.mjs (Apply now waits for the deck to reach 02 and the roll to finish; that one also failed on the old files). lab/deck-check.mjs read the removed numerals and now reads the current stop's index.

Checks: programme-check all passed, deck-check ok (0 to 8 in order at both widths, swipe and Change link), bespoke-check all UI cases match, bespoke-phone clean (.dk-fee still found), bespoke-twoway-check all passed, bespoke-howlong-check all ok, bespoke-treat-check all passed, bespoke-summary-check all ok; sync-header and sync-footer --check ok on every page.
Screenshots: lab/bespoke-bar2/ (p0, p1, p4, p8 at each size; slide-1440-f0 to f6 and slide-390-f0 to f6 with slide-1440-strip.png and slide-390-strip.png; fee-rolling-1440/390; jump-mid, focus and widest at each size; check-output.txt). Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-bar2.{html,css,js}.bak, lab/deck-check.before-bar2.mjs.bak, lab/bespoke-twoway-check.before-bar2.mjs.bak, lab/bespoke-summary-check.before-bar2.mjs.bak. Not committed or pushed.

## Bespoke Programmes: every screen fits (TJ, 12 Oct 2026)
TJ's decisions (phase 2 of the redesign): "no internal scrolling and no continuous scroll"; every step fits one screen with economic spacing, the bar at the foot; "steps need not be numbered"; the title can be resized, reshaped and repositioned; break things into more steps where a screen will not fit. The fit check and release follow the Tailored rule TJ approved there. Everything below the decisions is Claude's execution unless marked.

Built:
- **No step numbers.** The mono 01 to 07 beside every step title is gone (and its CSS). Step titles are clamp(1.6rem, min(3.2vw, 5.2vh), 2.8rem) on desktop and clamp(1.5rem, 7vw, 2.1rem) on phones; the gaps under them and under the lead paragraph take vh terms.
- **The start screen.** The three 01 to 03 / 04 to 06 / 07 rows are gone; the "It isn't a quote or a booking" note stays. The Quentin line, the capitals and the paragraph size from the window height too (vh in their clamps). The photograph is as tall as the deck allows (desktop: 4:5 capped at the deck's height; phones: it takes whatever height the copy leaves, so at 360x640 it is a short band at the top, the card merged upward into it as before).
- **More screens (17 pages, was 9).** Recovery treatments is two pages on every device, both titled Recovery treatments (the intro, herbal bath, belly binding and postpartum massage; then body scrub, foot reflexology, facial massage, herbal foot treatment, thermal blanket). What matters most is two pages (three priorities each, the second carrying the count line). The summary is seven pages: Your programme (the fee, its notes, the photograph), Your programme (what you have built, the first four areas with the restore link), Your programme (the last three areas), Your days (the day strip), Always included, Care budget (the budget and its recommender), Book (ready to talk, the links, the legal line). No new copy; every input, name, data-count and hook is unchanged. [data-summary] moved from the summary block to the deck element (the areas and Change links now sit on two pages; the script still finds it with the same selector).
- **Deviation from the brief (Claude, measured):** the brief asked for three summary screens. Measured on a phone with everything on (30 days, night care, every priority, a budget with two recommendations), the fee block with what you have built needed 630 to 760px against a 615px deck at 390x844 (411px at 360x640); the day strip with always included needed 917px; the budget with ready to talk needed 746px. So the summary pages were split further, and What matters most (505px at 360x640) as well.
- **Joined screens (Claude).** So a laptop does not get thin half empty screens, from 1024px wide a page marked data-join shares the screen of the page before it, two halves side by side (each half's content up to 560px, the pair on the usual 1080px measure). Joined: the two What matters most pages, the two built pages, Your days with Always included, Care budget with Book. A half without its own title (data-join="align") hides its repeated title and the script starts its rows level with the first row beside it. So the bar has 13 stops from 1024px wide and 17 on phones; the stops are screens, and data-pair-title names a joined screen (Your days; Book for the last). Recovery treatments stays two screens on every device, as briefed.
- **Step names for TJ to approve or rename (Claude's, from existing headings or the page's own words):** Your days, Always included, Care budget, Book. Your programme is used for three pages, Recovery treatments and What matters most for two each.
- **Next's label:** Begin on the start; See my programme on the screen before the first summary screen; Next between summary screens; Book a call (the ember button) only on the last screen. The Change links keep data-goto as page indexes (1, 3, 4, 5, 8 in the new order); deck.go now takes a page index and goes to the screen that page is on, so the budget's Apply (page 2) and the Change links land right at every width.
- **The brief text** (the mono block that Copy my programme summary copies) is no longer shown on the page; it stays in the DOM and is still what the button copies. It repeated the areas and did not fit (Claude, for TJ to approve).
- **The fit check (js/build-your-programme.js, deckController).** When the deck is built, after fonts, on load, on a resize (any width change, or a height change of 40px or more), on every form change or input, and when a page's content changes size (ResizeObserver), each page's content is measured against the deck in the held frame. A page that overflows takes .is-tight (closer padding and gaps, smaller titles and paragraphs, body text never under 13px; on phones the day strip's marks get shorter). If a page still overflows, or the window is under 500px tall, the frame is released: .dk.is-free (and body.dk-is-free) applies the flowing page rules from lab/flow-attempt (the window scrolls, the pages are sections down it, one fixed aurora, the bar fixed at the foot following the section being read, Back, Next and Change gliding the window with the page's own ease). Held is the default; .dk-page is overflow hidden, never auto. .dk carries data-fit (fits, tight, free) and data-over (the pages that forced a release). data-fit-hold on the root stops the check (for lab/bespoke-fit-probe.mjs only). The refit is debounced by a 30ms timer, not a frame request: in headless Chrome a frame request was seen to stall after screenshots.

Measured (lab/bespoke-fit-check.mjs; three states each: default; full = 30 days, night, everything on, every priority, the tallest budget recommendation; bare = 5 days, no meals, every treatment off, so the floor notice and Restore show):

| Size | Deck | Screens | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1440x900 | 722 | 13 | all 17 pages fit, all three states |
| 390x844 | 615 | 17 | all fit; Your days tight with 30 days (417 of 615 once tight) |
| 1835x605 | 427 | 13 | all fit; Care budget tight with two recommendations (374 of 427) |
| 360x640 | 411 | 17 | held; tight: both Recovery treatments pages (389 and 355; 409 bare) and Book (336), and with 30 days Your days (411 of 411) and Care budget (410) |
| 1280x480 | | | released (under 500px tall): the window scrolls (6369px), no element scrolls on its own, the bar fixed at the foot, Next lands step 01 under the header (0px), the bar reads Massage rhythm when scrolled there, Change (Meals) glides back to Meals |

No element scrolls on its own and no sideways overflow at any size or state. The start screen fits at all four sizes untight.

Checks: programme-check all passed; deck-check walks every screen (13 at 1440, 17 at 390; labels and fee as expected; swipe and Change link); bespoke-check all UI cases match; bespoke-phone clean; bespoke-twoway-check all passed; bespoke-howlong-check all ok; bespoke-treat-check all passed; bespoke-summary-check ALL OK; bespoke-bar2-check all passed (324 checks: prev, current and next visible with 13 and 17 stops, the slide, the definite ends, the widest bar on See my programme); lab/bespoke-release-check.mjs all passed (the release, and the five Change links at 1440x900 and 390x844); sync-header and sync-footer --check ok. Updated tests: deck-check (walks to the last stop, clicks scrub on the second treatments screen, Change from the screen that holds it), bespoke-summary-check (summary screens photographed in turn, Change indexes 1, 3, 4, 5, 8 mapped to screens), bespoke-bar2-check (stop count from the stops, See my programme found by its label). ## Bespoke Programmes: one way to choose, toggles (TJ, 13 Oct 2026)
TJ: the sliding line choices were too abstract. Every choice on every step now uses the same mechanism, so once she has done the first step she knows them all. No cards and no borders round the rows (TJ and the client both dislike bordered cards). The progress line stays only in the bar at the foot.
- **The row (TJ's direction, Claude's details):** the option's name and its one line on the left, a note on the right where there is one (sessions, times, Standard), a toggle at the end; only a hairline between rows. Off: words in ink-mute, a grey outlined toggle (#8F8480, 3.2 to 1 on the canvas). On: the name in ember, the line in ink-soft, the toggle filled ember with its knob slid across, animated over 240ms. Ember, not the paler corals, because only ember reads at this size on the canvas (4.9 to 1; coral-deep 3.9, coral-mid 3.2), which TJ agreed.
- **Pick one steps** (Live-in or daytime, How long, Day or night, Meals, Massage rhythm) are radios under the toggle: turning one on turns the rest off, and the one that is on cannot be turned off. **Pick any steps** (Recovery treatments, What matters most) are checkboxes. A small line under each step's title says which: Choose one, Choose any (Claude's words, TJ agreed). Meals is a pick one pair, Yes, cook for us / No meals, so it is always answered.
- **Always included** treatments (herbal bath, belly binding): lit in ember with INCLUDED where the toggle would be.
- **Defaults on (TJ):** the page opens with the defaults already on (Live-in care, 5 days, Daytime care, Yes cook for us, the treatments on, the standard massage rhythm), so the bar shows a real fee from the start and no step blocks Next. What matters most starts all off, as before. The pricing engine is untouched: every input keeps its name and value.
- **Files:** build-your-programme.html (steps 01 to 07 rewritten as .tgl lists of .tg rows by lab/to-toggles.py), js/build-your-programme.js (the rhythm rows' template, priorities() read by input name, align() looks for .tg), css/build-your-programme.css (the ONE WAY TO CHOOSE block at the end; the old .dkl, .dkd, .dk-dot, .treat, .rhythm and .prio rules are left in place but no longer used). Backups: lab/build-your-programme.before-toggles.{html,css,js}.bak and lab/*.before-toggles.mjs.bak.
- **Checked:** lab/toggle-check.mjs (26 rows, no old controls left, the fee follows the toggles, a pick one toggle cannot be turned off, night care still live-in only, the rhythm still off without massage, the priority count still shows; no overflow or errors at 1440 and 390), programme-check all passed, bespoke-fit-check all fit (the 360x640 bare state needed 421 of 411px on the first treatments screen; the tight rows were brought in by 2px each), bespoke-summary-check ALL OK, deck-check no errors. bespoke-treat-check, bespoke-howlong-check and bespoke-twoway-check tested the old controls and no longer apply. Tight pages keep their own spacing (no room to spare). Joined screens (from 1024px) cannot centre apart or the two halves would part company, so a new centre() in the script, run after align() in fit(), gives the pair one shared offset (--join-shift, half the room left by the taller half) and both halves take it. Phones are unchanged.

(the client's own earlier heading); one tap, not toggles and Continue; after Not yet, Hold this price or Check my dates by due date, then Done, as built; on phones everything shows without a scroll unless she opens the expand; "Not yet, email me my programme" and the Always included line are fine for now (TJ will review all copy with Amidat at delivery).

Left: INDICATIVE FEE and the rolling fee, the introductory pricing line, the built line ([data-built-title], moved from the old built screen) with Change (back to Live-in or daytime), then a details expand holding the days chart ([data-shape], moved from Your days) and one Always included line (Claude's wording from the old list). Right: Ready to talk it through?, the client's lead, the ember Book a call (Calendly with the programme and due date, as chat 4) and Not yet, email me my programme, which goes to the close. Copy my programme summary sits quietly under them. Next is hidden on this screen and the close is reached only through Not yet (Next, the arrow keys, the stops and a swipe all stop here).

## Bespoke Programmes: the due date calendar (TJ, 13 Oct 2026)

What TJ asked: replace the due date field on the Bespoke Programmes page with a calendar that looks identical to the single treatments booking calendar. Date only (no time step), no limits either way: the previous and next month arrows are never disabled, every day is selectable, today is marked bold and underlined, it opens on the current month (or the month of a chosen date), one day pressed at a time. The chosen date is written out under the calendar ("Monday 15 February 2027", en-GB, no comma). The note under the title now reads "Already had your baby? Choose the birth date."

How it works: a hidden input (id "due", name "due") stays the single source of truth. Tapping a day writes YYYY-MM-DD into it and dispatches an input event, so dueDate(), update('due') and deck.dueChanged() (Next unblocking) are untouched in behaviour. dueDate() no longer caps the year at 2000 to 2100. The look is re-created in build-your-programme.css as .due-cal, .due-cal__head, __month, __step, __grid, __wd and .due-day, mirroring booking.css's .bk-cal and .bk-day with the same tokens. Computed styles were compared against the live booking calendar at 390px wide and match on size, border, radius, colour, type and gaps (the only differences are the width of the calendar, below).

Files: index/build-your-programme.html, index/css/build-your-programme.css, index/js/build-your-programme.js. Untouched: build-your-programme-pricing.js, booking.js, booking.css, header and footer partials. Check scripts: every lab script that did p.fill('#due', ...) now sets the hidden input and dispatches input (bespoke-fit-check, bespoke-summary-check, decide-check, deck-check, funnel-close-check, header-centre-check, sum-study, toggle-check); lab/funnel-due-check.mjs was rewritten to test the calendar itself (24 presses back, 48 forward, a past date, words under the calendar, Next unblocked).

Backups in lab/: build-your-programme.before-due-cal.{html,css,js}.bak, DESIGN.before-due-cal.md.bak, and <name>.before-due-cal.mjs.bak for each of the nine scripts above (including funnel-due-check).

Screenshots in lab/funnel/: due-cal-empty-* and due-cal-chosen-* at 1440x900, 390x844 and 360x640, plus due-cal-booking-ref-* and due-cal-ours-* for the side by side with the single treatments calendar.

Deviations (Claude):
1. Width. Booking's calendar fills a 680px column at 1440; here the calendar is capped at 22rem (20rem when the page is tight) so the screen stays short enough to fit. At 390px both are 342px wide and identical.
2. The note sits above the calendar, under the lead line, not below it; the chosen date is the line under the calendar.
3. The fit rule. The frame releases for the whole deck if any one page overflows, so the due screen has to fit for the tallest month, a six row month such as May 2027. On .is-tight the head, weekday row and note close up, the day buttons are a fixed 40px, and for a six row month on the shortest screens (360x640, 1835x605) the day buttons drop to 36px with a 1px gap. This is below TJ's 40px minimum for phones; the alternative was releasing the whole frame into scroll mode. Five row months keep 40px.
4. bespoke-fit-check.mjs now fills 2027-05-15 (a six row month) instead of 2027-03-15 so it measures the worst case.
5. "Today" is the visitor's local date, not London time as in booking.

Fit, due screen (content height against deck height, bespoke-fit-check, default state): six row month (May 2027, what the check measures): 1440x900 612 of 722 (743 full and bare); 390x844 596 of 615; 1835x605 394 of 427 (448 full and bare); 360x640 409 of 411. Five row month (current month, October 2026), measured earlier in the build: 1440x900 562, 390x844 547, 1835x605 396, 360x640 402.

## Homepage: hero, bath figures and the door handoff (TJ, 14 Oct 2026)
- **Hero (TJ):** the photograph is What We Do's afternoon sofa image (care.webp). The header ends on a clean edge with no fade (the top fade is gone), and the crop is centred top to bottom (object-position 62% 50%). Book a consultation is gone from the hero; no comma after "care"; no full stop after "too". The script line is 10 percent larger than the headline at every size. Entrance: line one, line two, script, lede, 1s each, .16s apart, from .2s (the What We Do opening rhythm).
- **Bath (TJ; figures and Quentin wording chosen by TJ from Claude's options):** the slogan is gone. In the steam above her head, centred: "In thirty days", then 360 hours of care, 90 family meals cooked, 30 restorative treatments, counting up on the climb's own scroll (lands before the pin lets go, reverses on the way back). Lower right over the bath: "Your recovery:" in the figures' label style at 1.04rem (30 percent larger), its midpoint on the left edge of "Well deserved" in Quentin. All three figures are derived from Amidat's own promises (7am to 7pm, three meals a day, a treatment every day) and still need her confirmation before launch; 360 counts the 7am to 7pm window including her break.
- **Door handoff (TJ):** the bedroom handover and the flight round the outside are cut from the house film, which now starts at the closed front door (frame 99 of the old cut, 3.3s). Uncut originals: assets/video/home-flight.with-handover.mp4 and home-flight-m.with-handover.mp4; first poster home-door.webp. home-flight.js keeps every time in the old timeline's numbers and shifts them with CUT; TRACK_VH shrinks by the same ratio so a vh of scroll moves the film as far as before; a 0.7vh LEAD holds the closed door before scrubbing starts.
- **The smoke (TJ, second build):** everything moves with the scroll, never against it. TJ rejected the first build (the bath sinking back down and a flat white screen). Now home.js doorHandoff(): once "Well deserved" has landed the smoke rises over the bottom of the held frame, the bath lets go and rises out under the header, and a smoke bank climbs just ahead of its bottom edge (about a tenth of a window), so the house film arriving behind it is never seen. The bank is assets/img/fog-bank.webp, built locally from fog.webp: the plate's own feathered front squeezed into the top 300 rows, then the plate flipped over a base that is solid from row 480 to the bottom (2600). The plate alone peaks at 72 percent and showed the house through it; a longer feather whited the bath out before it could be seen to rise; a short solid body brought the bank's bottom edge on screen while clearing. A veil in the smoke's own white closes the last of the bath; "From the moment we arrive" (Instrument Serif capitals) holds in it while the film arrives; then the veil goes, the bank drifts up and fades, and the door dissolves through the smoke before scrolling opens it. Every layer is two elements (inner rises, outer clears) so the two timelines never drive the same property and scrolling back up restores each state. Positions are keyed to the bath's bottom edge and the flight's top, so the phone works without its own numbers. .act--bath is 335vh (phone 365vh). Slower and later (TJ): the finished frame with the figures holds about 0.55 of a window after the count lands; the smoke then rises over the still held bath for 0.8 of a window before it lets go (doorHandoff keyed from the edge at 1.8 windows, was 1.3). The line now stays through the clearing and the door opening and dissolves 0.35 to 1.0 of a window after the lead (about 0.6 to 1.9s into the film), with a soft radial wash of the canvas behind it in its own wrapper so it reads on the door; on phones it sits at 30svh, above the caption card. Third pass (TJ): "Your recovery: Well deserved" is never under the smoke. The bath is sticky, so it is its own stacking layer; home.js moves .bath__copy into a fixed .bath__float layer (z 41, above the smoke at 40, under the header) and shifts it by the bath's own offset every frame, so it holds and rises with the photograph. It dissolves as her head leaves the top (bath edge 0.45 to 0.3 of a window) while "From the moment we arrive" dissolves in over the same stretch (phone: 0.33 to 0.2, after the copy has risen past the line's spot). Then (TJ): the line's fade in is slower and starts earlier so it overlaps the copy leaving: bath edge 0.7 to 0.3 (phone 0.5 to 0.2); its fade out over the door is unchanged. The white is short: the veil fills from edge 0.5 to 0.1, and LEAD_VH and DOOR_CLEAR_VH are 0.4 (were 0.7), so the door is clear 0.4 of a window after the film arrives. Checked at 1440x900 and 390x844 forward and back, no errors. Next (TJ): the progression bar replaces the glass panels in the house film, starting at "From the moment we arrive", then the rooms.
- **The curtain (TJ approved Fable's recommendation, fourth pass):** the white screen is gone entirely. The house film is pulled up under the bath (.flight--home margin-top minus the bath frame's height, .act--bath z 2 over .flight--home z 1), so its stage is held on the closed door from the moment the bath lets go, and the bath lifts off it like a curtain. The smoke is now a band, fog.webp with a flipped copy under it (each 60svh tall, 130vw wide), densest in the middle, kept on the bath's bottom edge by one frame loop in doorHandoff() (lift.off rises it from 0.7 of a window below the edge to just above it while the bath is held; lift.away pulls it clear over the last 0.4 so a phone settling on the door has none left over it). The bath's bottom edge feathers out as it lets go (mask over --bath-feather, 0 to 35 percent of the window) so no straight line crosses the door. LEAD_VH and DOOR_LEAD_VH are 1: the door holds closed until the bath has fully gone, which is also where a phone settles. fog-bank.webp and the veil are deleted. Copy and line timings are unchanged. Then (TJ): the film is already moving as the smoke clears. The files are re-cut to open 1.0s before the door (frame 69 of the old cut, just after its cut from the flight round the house, where the camera holds on the front of the house and pushes in; CUT = 69/30, DOOR = 1.0). A first cut at frame 81 with an ease from still looked frozen for half the lift (TJ). Over the lead, while the bath lifts, home-flight.js plays that push half steady and half eased (time = DOOR * (0.5x + 0.5x^n), n set so it meets the door at the track's own speed, no jolt): 1 to 2 frames per tenth of a window at first, 5 at the door and lands on the closed door exactly where it used to hold; from the door on every scroll point and frame is as before (track 0 is the door). Reduced motion and no JS put the film back in plain flow (margin 0). Known: the Arrival card and the film's Enquire button show through the dissolve as the bath lifts; on phones "Well deserved" crosses the line's spot as it fades.
- Backups: lab/*.before-door-start.*.bak, lab/*.before-curtain.*.bak, lab/*.before-smoke-clears.js.bak, lab/*.before-bath-feather.*.bak, lab/home-flight.before-preroll.js.bak, lab/home-flight.before-longer-glide.js.bak, lab/*.before-smoke-door.*.bak, lab/*.before-slower-smoke.*.bak, lab/*.before-copy-over-smoke.*.bak, lab/*.before-bath-*.bak, lab/*.before-hero-*.bak, lab/*.before-well-deserved.*.bak, lab/*.before-kicker-thirty.css.bak.

- **The route as a timeline (TJ, 14 Oct 2026):** The homepage house film's route no longer lists the six stops at once. It now behaves like the A Day With Us timeline turned on its side and driven by film time. The circle holds still at a fixed x (30 percent of the route width on desktop, 14 percent on phones) and the line is drawn only up to it, nothing ahead. A track of stops slides left beneath the circle at 0.2 of the screen width per film second (widened on phones so stops are always further apart than a stop's words). Each stop is born when film time reaches its room's crossfade (or its settled frame, if earlier, so a phone's snapped frame always shows its stop; the window at the midpoint where the scene switches): its dot grows on the line, its name rises out of a mask above and its words out of a mask below. Going back in time reverses it, and passed stops are carried off left and dissolve in a mask fade at the route's edges. It is horizontal at every width, low in the frame on desktop (line 20svh up from the bottom) and in the lower blurred area on phones (line at 60svh). Same six names and words, same fade in and out with film time, same soft wash behind. Backups in lab: index.before-route-timeline.html.bak, house-flight.before-route-timeline.css.bak, home-flight.before-route-timeline.js.bak.
- **The route centred, with stems (TJ, 14 Oct 2026):** The route is now one fixed line, centred across the foot of the film (up to 1120px wide between the gutters, 20svh up from the bottom on desktop, at 60svh on phones), with both ends dissolving in a mask. The whole line is a faint hairline, with a solid Ink fill to "now" and the circle at its tip. Film time moves "now" along the line, piecewise linear between six hand placed stops (Arrival .08, Kitchen .24, Living Room .42, Nursery .52, Treatment Room .72, The Window .88 of the line), reaching each stop's dot exactly at its birth. Each dot grows when the fill reaches it and stays. The current room grows a 1px stem upward from its dot and its name and words rise out of masks beside the stem, on the side where the picture is quietest (right for the first three, left for the last three on desktop; on phones the side is automatic, left half reads right and right half reads left). When the camera moves on, the words mask back down and the stem retracts. A soft radial wash sits behind the line and behind the current words. The sliding track version it replaces is backed up. Each dot is born at the start of its window. Nothing animates on its own clock any more: every paint writes --p (stem, wash and name, rising over the first 0.3 s of a window and falling over the last 0.3 s), --pw (the words, 0.08 s behind) and --d (the dot, growing over 0.15 s after birth) onto the stop, and the CSS reads them with no transitions, so scrolling stops the ramps and scrolling back runs them back. - **The route: gliding track, leaders, no wash (TJ, 14 Oct 2026):** on desktop (768px and up) the circle now holds still at the centre of the line. The hairline runs the full width with both ends masked, and the Ink fill runs from the left end to the circle and never changes. Progress is shown by a track of dots gliding left beneath the circle: each dot is born at the circle at its window's start and drifts left at half the line's width per 4.5 s of film, dissolving in the left mask, so only the faint hairline lies ahead of the circle. The wash is gone on desktop (both the line's wash and the words' wash). The current room's words sit still on a plain part of its frame, and a 1px Ink leader (one SVG polyline per room, pathLength 1, drawn with --p from the dot outward) rises from the moving dot to the name's row and turns to the near edge of the name. Because a dot sweeps the whole left half of the line during a long window, the long rooms keep their words right of the centre so the leader always meets their left edge and never runs through the words; Arrival and The Window have short windows, so they sit upper left, right aligned, with the dot always to their right. Positions (fractions of the stage, ty is the top of the words): Arrival tx .25 ty .18 right aligned, on the plain brick wall clear of the door; Kitchen tx .555 ty .115 left aligned, on the cream wall cabinets and hood above the cook's head; Living Room tx .515 ty .105 left aligned and 24ch wide, on the terracotta wall left of the carer's head; Nursery tx .69 ty .115 left aligned and 21ch wide, on the plain wall between the arch's mobile and the bookshelf, narrow so the camera's pan never brings the books under it; Treatment Room tx .535 ty .115 left aligned, on the curtain and top window panes above the practitioner's head; The Window tx .26 ty .2 right aligned, in the open sky upper left. - **What Is Confinement fades in over the window (TJ, 14 Oct 2026):** the hand over from the house film is pinned and starts as soon as the house has gone (TJ: the wait on the sky was too long and the fade switched on like a light; before that, the section was still emerging when the testimonials came into shot). The pin starts at film time 24.0, after the last frame with any house, roof or tree (23.7 to 23.8 at 1440 x 900, earlier at 390 x 844; none from 23.9 at either size), and the film's last stretch, 24.0 to its end at 24.68, still plays on scroll under the start of the emerge with its own dissolve to the canvas, so the sky is turning white as the section appears (--blend 0.14 at the pin's start, 1 by a quarter of the way into the emerge). The route fades out by 23.5, the last frame with the roof in it, so its words are gone before the section appears. Mechanism: home-flight.js gives back that last stretch of track plus the stage's own window at the foot of the flight section (a negative bottom margin) and marks the act .is-handover (above the film, its stage at least a window tall less its top padding, so the testimonials below never show), so the act's top reaches the window's top at 24.0 while the film's stage stays held to the end of the track; home.js (actTurn) pins the act there with ScrollTrigger (pin, pinSpacing) for 2.2 windows: the emerge, a linear opacity 0 to 1 over 1.0 window as a whole (ease none, no dead start, so it reads as a steady emergence from the first scroll), then a hold of 1.2 windows fully in. The fade is a timeline scrubbed on the pin itself (scrub 0.7), so it always catches up with the scroll; nothing below can come up while the act is pinned, and if a very fast scroll reaches the end of the pin before the scrub has caught up, the pin's onLeave finishes it, so the act is always fully in when the page moves on. The heading's line assembly and the copy's entrance start at about 0.05 opacity and run for 1.2 s, so they rise with the fade rather than switching on part way through it. Every earlier scroll point and room frame is where it was. On a phone the exit scene settles at the pin's start, 24.0, and never inside the pin. Reduced motion and no JS: no pin, no overlap, the film and route run as before and the sections stack. Backups: lab/*.before-confinement-fade, before-confinement-beats, before-confinement-white, before-confinement-pin and before-confinement-tight (.bak), for index.html, house-flight.css, home-flight.js and home.js.

- **Route copy (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** The route now has five stops: Arrival, Kitchen, Living Room, Nursery and Treatment Room. The small room names are gone from the four rooms, and each large room title is now that stop's button, so it is the click and focus target (Arrival keeps its small name and stem). Treatment gained the large title "Daily treatments to restore you" with new words, and The Window is no longer a route stop (its film scene stays for timing). Backups in lab: index.before-route-copy.html.bak, house-flight.before-route-copy.css.bak and home-flight.before-route-copy.js.bak.
- **The route runs to What Is Confinement? (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** the progression line no longer fades before the white; it stays on screen through the sky, the white, the emerge and the hold, and dissolves only after the confinement pin lets go, scrubbed over 0.4 of a window as the testimonials come up, and back on scroll up. A sixth, final stop sits at 0.9 of the line: a dot only, with no title, words or stem, because the confinement section's own heading is its text. Its dot is born as the section starts to emerge (the pin's start, film 24.0), and the fill runs on from the treatment room to it, on film time up to the pin's start (to 0.863) and then on the pin's scrubbed emerge, reaching the dot exactly as the emerge ends; from then on the line is complete. The treatment stop's dot, window and words are unchanged, but the circle now travels a little slower after it (about 38px further left at the treatment rest at 1440) because the line now ends at the new stop instead of the line's end. So the route can outlive the film's held stage, home-flight.js moves it into a fixed layer the same size as the stage (.route-layer, z 3: above the film and the pinned section, below the header); the foot wash stays with the film. The pin's timeline in home.js feeds its emerge to the route, and a second scrubbed trigger after the pin feeds the dissolve. Reduced motion and no JS keep the route in the stage with its old fade at the film's end, and no final stop. On a phone the line crosses the confinement section's last paragraph during the hold (open). Backups: lab/*.before-route-to-confinement.*.bak for index.html, house-flight.css, home-flight.js and home.js.
- **The confinement hand over: the real bug (Claude, 15 Oct 2026):** seven rounds of retuning missed a load order race. home.js boots on document.fonts.ready and actTurn() decided whether to pin the act by reading the is-handover class, which home-flight.js (loaded after home.js) adds. With the fonts cached, as in TJ's Chrome, fonts.ready can resolve before home-flight.js has run, so no pin and no fade were ever created and the act scrolled up at full opacity through the route line (TJ's screenshots). Headless checks fetch the fonts fresh every time, so the race was never lost there and every build "passed". Fix: actTurn() decides from the same facts as home-flight.js (reduced motion off and a [data-flight] present) and adds the class itself, so the order no longer matters; home-flight.js also runs layout() on ScrollTrigger refreshInit so pins are measured against current heights. Reproduced with fonts.ready stubbed resolved (scratchpad race.mjs): before, no pin, opacity 1; after, pinned and fading. Backups lab/home.before-handover-race.js.bak, lab/home-flight.before-handover-race.js.bak. Lesson for every scroll build on this site: test with fonts.ready already resolved and with a real wheel, not only scrollTo.
- **The line leaves with the act (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** once complete, the route line belongs to What Is Confinement: after the pin lets go it no longer stays fixed and fades over the testimonials, it travels up and off with the act's text and photograph. home.js actTurn() shifts the fixed route layer by the act's own offset each frame (0 while the act is below or pinned, its negative top after), looking the layer up lazily because home-flight.js creates it and can run later (the fonts.ready race). The scrubbed route dissolve (ROUTE_OUT_VH) is removed. Checked at 1680x980 with fonts.ready stubbed resolved and fresh, and 390x844, with a real wheel down and back: the line keeps a constant distance from the act's top (843 px desktop, 506 px phone), returns to place on scroll back, no errors. The race fix from earlier today is untouched. Backup lab/home.before-line-scrolls-away.js.bak.
- **One title style on the line (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** every stop's title, Arrival and Amidat's room titles alike, is in Arrival's face (Montserrat 500, .16em spaced capitals) at the size half way between Arrival's old .8rem and the room titles' old clamp(1.35rem, 1.7vw, 1.9rem): --route-title on .route, 20.7px at 1680, 18.6px at 1440; phones half way between .74rem and 1.15rem, 15.1px at 390. Measured identical across all five titles at each size, no errors. Known: at 1680 "A living room that stays calm" wraps with "Calm" alone on the second line. - **Care rail pin measured stale (Claude, 15 Oct 2026):** TJ's screenshot showed the Four Kinds of Care rail already panning while the testimonials were still on screen. Cause: ScrollTrigger refreshes triggers in priority order, the rail's pin has refreshPriority 2 and the confinement pin had none, so the rail was measured before the confinement pin's 2.2 window spacer existed and its start was stale; the two pins overlapped by about 500px. Fix: the confinement pin gets refreshPriority 2 as well (equal priorities then sort by position), so it is measured first. After: the rail's pin starts 1665px after the confinement pin ends (the testimonials scrolling by) and the rail first moves with the care section's top at the window top, with fonts cached and fresh at 1680x980; phones have no rail pin. Backup lab/home.before-pin-order.js.bak.
- **Four Kinds of Care: the progression line (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** the site's line device under the care rail, desktop only (the phone rail is stacked, no travel). Built by actPan() inside the pinned stage, centred at the bottom, min(720px, page width), in white on the grey silk (the footer's colours on the same ground): a faint track, the fill and the circle at its tip, a dot per card once reached, and each card's own title beneath its dot (Montserrat .74rem capitals, normal spacing, the current one full white). The fill is mapped from the rail's own position: it reaches a card's stop as that card's centre crosses 60 percent of the window, so the line and the cards always agree; a tap on a name scrolls to that card. Checked at 1680x980 with fonts cached and 1440x900 fresh: fill 0 to 1 through the pin, the current name follows the card in view, tapping Meals lands on Meals, no overlap with the card text, no errors. Backups lab/home.before-care-line.js.bak, lab/site.before-care-line.css.bak.
- **Care line glides with the cards (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** replaces the fixed 720px bar. The line now spans the full width (the stage's edge mask fades its ends), the circle holds the screen centre with the fill from the left edge to it, and the stops sit on a list home.js moves with the rail, each dot under its card's centre and the card's title beneath it, so they travel with the photographs and pass the circle as each card reaches the centre (house film manner). The hairline under each card title, above the photograph, is removed in this section. Measured at 1680 (fonts cached) and 1440 at five points through the pin: every dot within 1px of its card's centre, circle at the screen centre, the current name follows, tap on Meals lands on Meals, no errors; phones unchanged (stacked, no line). Backups lab/home.before-care-line-glide.js.bak, lab/site.before-care-line-glide.css.bak.
- **Care names once, above the line (TJ, 15 Oct 2026):** the title above each photograph is visually hidden (kept for screen readers and for home.js, which reads the names from it), and each card's name now rides above the line with its dot instead of below it, so the name is said once as the line progresses. Checked at 1680 (fonts cached) and 1440: dots still within 1px of their cards, tap works, no errors. Backup lab/site.before-care-names-above.css.bak.
- **The transitions, three faults found and fixed (Claude, 16 Oct 2026):** TJ reported the fly-through line overlaying where it should not and transitions moving where they should not. Measured through the whole sequence at 1680x980 and 390x844 with cached fonts and real wheel and touch input. (1) Stacking: when the route was lifted into a fixed layer it went to z 3, above the bath (z 2) and the smoke, so the line and words drew over the bath photograph while it lifted off the door. The layer is now z 2 and the bath z 3; verified the element under the line at every stage (bath, then film, then confinement). (2) The phone film could not be scrolled at all: the rest settle fired 120ms after any scroll event and snapped to the nearest rest, and one swipe is less than half the gap between rests, so every swipe went straight back to the door. The settle now only arms on touch, waits 420ms of true idle, and only settles when within 0.9s of a rest; wheel input never settles. Touch now runs the film 1.0 to 11.9s over 14 swipes, wheel 1.5 to 8.5s. (3) While the confinement act holds, the line's fixed height crossed its last paragraph on phones and the photograph on a short desktop window (1280x720). home.js now measures the act and, once it is at the top, shifts the line into the clear band under the content (phones: the gap between the last paragraph and the photograph; desktop: below the lower of the two), reading the natural line height once per layout so the maths never feeds on its output; the line then leaves with the act as before. Hold sweep at 1280, 1440, 1680, 1920 and 390: the line crosses neither text nor photograph at any width. No errors anywhere. Backups lab/*.before-route-stack.css.bak, lab/home-flight.before-settle-fix.js.bak, lab/home.before-phone-line-shift.js.bak.
- **Late image loads broke the pins on TJ's machine (Claude, 16 Oct 2026):** TJ, desktop, after a hard reload: scrolling past What Is Confinement skipped the testimonials straight to the grey section, and the line jumped, until he scrolled back and forth. Cause: the site loads from Google Drive, so the window load event (every image in) fires seconds after scrolling has started; boot()'s goToAnchor then scrolled to 0 and refreshed, which in a slowed reproduction threw the page to the top mid scroll and re-measured the pins under the reader. Headless loads images instantly, so every earlier check missed it. Fix in home.js: once the reader has moved (wheel, touch, key or pointer), the load routine only refreshes in place (ScrollTrigger keeps the scroll position); and a ResizeObserver on the body plus a listener on every unfinished image triggers one debounced in place refresh whenever the document height changes. Reproduced with images delayed 6 and 12 seconds at 1680x980: before, the testimonials never showed and the page jumped to 0; after, the testimonials show and the care rail starts only at its section's top, the same as with instant loading. Earlier checks rerun clean. Backup lab/home.before-late-load.js.bak. Test rule: slow images (page.route delay) belong in every scroll check on this site, with cached fonts and a real wheel.

## Who Comes Into Your Home redesigned (TJ, 19 Oct 2026)

TJ: the page was flat, placeholder like and incoherent, with too much text in the hero; make it feel like What We Do without copying it, smaller and simple to read. TJ approved Claude's plan and said build it whole, best assumptions, placeholders where needed.

- Five beats, every word the client's (copy.json "specialist" and the FAQ's Our Specialists group). (plan Claude's, approved by TJ)
  1. Hero: portrait with its opening, the title, "One dedicated person in your home, chosen with care." and one sentence, "The person who looks after you is the heart of your stay." The second paragraph and the checks list left the hero.
  2. Statement: "You will have the same specialist throughout, so the care feels personal from the first day to the last." Instrument Serif, sentence case, alone on the aurora, words rising one after another.
  3. Trained in our approach to recovery: her sentence held on the left; the photo stack on the right (hands-mother-baby, hands-treatments, hands-meals), each sliding up over the last as the one beneath settles back (scale .92, fade). First use of the photo stack from section 9. Glass captions use her own phrases: Mothers and newborns, Treatments and binding, Postpartum meals.
  4. Before anyone joins us (this page's own moment): her sentence on the left; the six checks in large capitals on the right on one fine line that draws down with the scroll, each check lighting Ember as the line reaches it, then cooling to ink.
  5. Before she arrives: s2-handoff wide with her sentence on frosted glass (glass sits below the photograph on phones), then three Our Specialists questions in the site's question rows, and a Frequently Asked Questions link to faqs.html#faq-specialists.
- Removed: the September article (old title and lede, Meet Your Specialist, You Meet Her First, Vetting and Safety, If It Is Not the Right Match, both Notes for Amidat, the massage photograph). That copy was not the client's, and Vetting and Safety contradicted her verified words.
- Placeholder: a comment in the markup marks where the specialist's name, background and years of experience go once Amidat answers SITE-PLAN questions 11 to 13. Nothing is shown until then.
- Files: css/who-comes.css and js/who-comes.js (new); specialist.css now pads the hero for .page--who; the body carries page--who. No other page touched.
